# Heroes

Journal: `quests/side_quest/sq018_jackie` · `SideQuest` · hash `2495848031`.

Journal premise: I don't usually give advice, but... fuck it, here goes. Don't make the same mistake I made – say goodbye to the people you love. You know what I mean, right? Jackie Welles was your best choom, and you don't find a lot of straight-up peeps like that in NC. Best thing you can do is remember them. Oh, and one more thing. If anyone asks, I didn't just say all that to you. Got it?

**Coverage: journal-linked.** 113 exact journal-linked nodes in 16 resources; 17 resources in the inspected dependency context.

Resource roles in that context: 15 content candidate, 2 repair. Role labels describe path/name evidence, not proof of runtime reachability.

## Required acceptance cases before reuse

Exercise normal completion and each named alternate outcome; interrupt and return at every scene handoff; start with spatial/item/actor predicates already satisfied; save/load across waits and joins; cancel parallel listeners; verify reward delivery once; and verify cleanup without changing the committed outcome. Apply the block-specific cases in the [expanded contracts](../all-quest-blocks.md). No installed playtest is claimed.

## Connected paths and supporting bindings

988 repeated typed/socket path signatures in this selected graph context; 181 distinct direct resource identities; 16 embedded graph handoffs. The full occurrence lists, concrete parameters, exact source pointers and external edges are retained in the checked evidence. Shared forward dependencies can serve several quests; these are context counts.
